    async function loadServer(job: Job): Promise<Server & { plan: any; gamePlan: any; osTemplate: any; node: any; user: any }> {
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({
        where: { id: job.serverId! },
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        include: { plan: true, gamePlan: true, osTemplate: true, node: true, user: { select: { id: true, email: true } } },
      });
      if (!server) throw new Error(`Server ${job.serverId} not found`);
      return server as any;
    }
    
    
    async function nodeFor(server: any) {
      const n =
        server.node ??
        (await db.node.findFirst({ where: { isPrimary: true } })) ??
        (await db.node.findFirst({ where: { enabled: true } }));
      if (!n) {
    
        return { conn: { get: pve.get, post: pve.post, put: pve.put, del: pve.del, node, waitForTask, nextVmid, vmStatus: (v: number) => pve.get(`/nodes/${node}/qemu/${v}/status/current`) } as any, row: null };
      }
      return { conn: pveFor({ apiUrl: n.apiUrl, pveNode: n.pveNode, tokenId: n.tokenId, tokenSecret: n.tokenSecret }), row: n };
    }
    
    
    function indentBlock(text: string, spaces: number): string {
      const pad = " ".repeat(spaces);
      return text.split("\n").map((l) => pad + l).join("\n");
    }
    
    
    
    function shipperInstallScript(serverId: string, token: string, nodeKind: "VPS" | "GAME"): string {
      const appUrl = process.env.APP_URL ?? "https://nxeon.cloud";
      const config = JSON.stringify({
        server_id: serverId,
        token,
        ingest_url: `${appUrl}/api/telemetry/ingest`,
        node_kind: nodeKind,
      });
      return [
        "mkdir -p /etc/nxeon",
        `cat > /etc/nxeon/shipper.json <<'NXTJSON'\n${config}\nNXTJSON`,
        "chmod 600 /etc/nxeon/shipper.json",
        `curl -fsSL "${appUrl}/dl/nxeon-shipper-linux-amd64" -o /usr/local/bin/nxeon-shipper.tmp && ` +
          "chmod +x /usr/local/bin/nxeon-shipper.tmp && mv /usr/local/bin/nxeon-shipper.tmp /usr/local/bin/nxeon-shipper",
        `curl -fsSL "${appUrl}/dl/nxeon-shipper.service" -o /etc/systemd/system/nxeon-shipper.service`,
        "systemctl daemon-reload && systemctl enable --now nxeon-shipper",
      ].join("\n");
    }
    
    function buildCloudInit(server: any, opts: { golden?: boolean } = {}): string {
      const addons = (server.addons ?? {}) as {
        malwareScanner?: boolean;
        dailyBackup?: boolean;
        docker?: boolean;
        claudeCode?: boolean;
      };
      const setup: string[] = [];
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const gameSetup = buildGameSetupScript(server);
      if (gameSetup) setup.push(gameSetup);
    
    
    
    
      if (addons.claudeCode !== false) {
    
    
        setup.push(...CLAUDE_CLI_INSTALL_STEPS);
      }
    
      if (addons.docker) setup.push(DOCKER_CLASSIC_STORE_SNIPPET, "{ command -v docker >/dev/null 2>&1 || curl -fsSL https://get.docker.com | sh; } && systemctl enable --now docker");
      if (addons.malwareScanner)
        setup.push("(apt-get install -y clamav clamav-daemon || dnf install -y clamav) && (systemctl enable --now clamav-freshclam || true)");
    
    
      if (server.__nxcloudSetup) setup.push(server.__nxcloudSetup);
    
    
    
    
    
      if (server.__telemetryToken) {
        const kind: "VPS" | "GAME" = server.gamePlanId ? "GAME" : "VPS";
        setup.push(shipperInstallScript(server.id, server.__telemetryToken, kind));
      }
    
      const script = ["#!/bin/bash", "set -x", ...setup].join("\n");
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const swapScript = [
        "#!/bin/bash",
        "# Nxeon: ensure a swapfile exists as an OOM safety net on small-RAM VMs.",
        "if swapon --show=NAME --noheadings 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then exit 0; fi",
        "[ -e /swapfile ] && exit 0",
        'MEM_MB=$(awk "/MemTotal/{print int(\\$2/1024)}" /proc/meminfo 2>/dev/null || echo 1024)',
        "SWAP_MB=$MEM_MB",
        '[ "$SWAP_MB" -lt 1024 ] && SWAP_MB=1024',
        '[ "$SWAP_MB" -gt 4096 ] && SWAP_MB=4096',
        "# Leave >2 GB free on / so the swapfile never fills the root disk.",
        'AVAIL_MB=$(df -Pm / | awk "NR==2{print \\$4}")',
        '[ -n "$AVAIL_MB" ] && [ "$AVAIL_MB" -lt $((SWAP_MB + 2048)) ] && SWAP_MB=$((AVAIL_MB - 2048))',
        '[ "$SWAP_MB" -lt 256 ] && exit 0',
        "fallocate -l ${SWAP_MB}M /swapfile 2>/dev/null || dd if=/dev/zero of=/swapfile bs=1M count=${SWAP_MB} 2>/dev/null || exit 0",
        "chmod 600 /swapfile",
        "mkswap /swapfile >/dev/null 2>&1 || exit 0",
        "swapon /swapfile 2>/dev/null || exit 0",
        "grep -q '^/swapfile ' /etc/fstab 2>/dev/null || echo '/swapfile none swap sw 0 0' >> /etc/fstab",
        "grep -q '^vm.swappiness' /etc/sysctl.conf 2>/dev/null || { echo 'vm.swappiness=10' >> /etc/sysctl.conf; sysctl -w vm.swappiness=10 >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }",
      ].join("\n");
    
      const welcome = [
        "# Welcome to your Nxeon server",
        "",
        "## Build anything with Claude Code — it's already installed",
        "",
        "1. You're already logged in. Just run:",
        "",
        "   claude",
        "",
        "2. Sign in once — either with your Claude subscription (Pro/Max) or an",
        "   Anthropic API key. See the step-by-step guide with screenshots at:",
        "",
        "   https://nxeon.cloud/dashboard/claude-code",
        "",
        "3. Then describe what you want in plain English, for example:",
        '   "Build a modern website for my coffee shop with a menu and contact form,',
        '    then run it and tell me the URL to preview it."',
        "",
        "Copy-paste starter prompts (spa site, booking app, REST API, Discord bot,",
        "and 20+ more) and how-to guides live in your portal at the link above.",
        "",
        "Happy building!",
      ].join("\n");
    
    
    
    
      const motd = [
        "#!/bin/sh",
        'if [ -t 1 ]; then',
        '  if command -v claude >/dev/null 2>&1 || [ -x /usr/local/bin/claude ]; then',
        '    printf "\\n  \\033[1mNxeon\\033[0m — Claude Code is pre-installed on this server.\\n"',
        '    printf "  Run \\033[1mclaude\\033[0m to start building. Guide: https://nxeon.cloud/dashboard/claude-code\\n"',
        "  else",
        '    printf "\\n  \\033[1mNxeon\\033[0m — install Claude Code:  curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash\\n"',
        '    printf "  Then run \\033[1mclaude\\033[0m. Guide: https://nxeon.cloud/dashboard/claude-code\\n"',
        "  fi",
        '  printf "  Full walkthrough: \\033[2m~/START-WITH-CLAUDE.md\\033[0m\\n\\n"',
        "fi",
      ].join("\n");
    
    
    
      return `#cloud-config
    ${cloudInitPackagesBlock(!!opts.golden)}ssh_pwauth: true
    disable_root: false
    write_files:
      - path: /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/60-nxeon.conf
        permissions: "0644"
        content: |
          PermitRootLogin yes
          PasswordAuthentication yes
      - path: /root/START-WITH-CLAUDE.md
        permissions: "0644"
        content: |
    ${indentBlock(welcome, 6)}
      - path: /etc/profile.d/nxeon-welcome.sh
        permissions: "0755"
        content: |

    async function claimVmidFloor(floor: number): Promise<number> {

    async function allocateFreshVmid(conn: any, backupStorage?: string): Promise<number> {
      const nextFree = await conn.nextVmid();
      const archived = await archivedVmids(conn, backupStorage);
    
    
      let taken = new Set<number>();
      try {
        const res: any[] = await conn.get("/cluster/resources", { type: "vm" });
        for (const r of res ?? []) if (Number.isInteger(r?.vmid)) taken.add(r.vmid);
      } catch {
        const vms: any[] = await conn.get(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu`);
        taken = new Set((vms ?? []).map((v: any) => Number(v?.vmid)).filter(Number.isInteger));
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const dbTaken = await db.server.findMany({ where: { vmid: { not: null } }, select: { vmid: true } });
      for (const r of dbTaken) if (r.vmid !== null) taken.add(r.vmid);
    
    
      for (let id = TEMPLATE_VMID_MIN; id <= TEMPLATE_VMID_MAX; id++) taken.add(id);
      for (const id of RESERVED_VMIDS) taken.add(id);
    
    
      const usedEver = await loadUsedVmids();
      const markBefore = await effectiveVmidMark(usedEver);
      for (const id of usedEver) taken.add(id);
    
    
      const candidate = await claimVmidFloor(
        freshVmidFloor(nextFree, dbTaken.map((r) => r.vmid).filter((v): v is number => v !== null))
      );
    
      const { vmid, skipped, skippedTaken, skippedArchived } = pickProvisionVmid({
        candidate,
        takenVmids: taken,
        archivedVmids: archived,
      });
    
    
    
      const stale = refuseStaleVmid({ vmid, effectiveMark: markBefore, used: new Set([...usedEver]) });
      if (stale) throw new Error(`refusing to provision: ${stale}`);
      if (vmid !== candidate) await bumpVmidHighWater(vmid);
      if (skipped.length > 0) {
    
    
    
    
        const parts: string[] = [];
        if (skippedTaken.length) parts.push(`${skippedTaken.join(", ")} already in use (live VM or Server row)`);
        if (skippedArchived.length) parts.push(`${skippedArchived.join(", ")} still holding a previous server's backup archives`);
        console.warn(`[worker] vmid skipped for provisioning — ${parts.join("; ")}; allocated ${vmid} instead`);
      }
      return vmid;
    }
    
    
    async function waitOutGameInstall(
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      probeServer: { id: string; rootPassword: string | null; rootPasswordEnc?: string | null; sshPort: number | null; dedicatedIp?: { address: string } | null; node?: { publicIp: string } | null },
      checkHost: string,
      checkPort: number,
      protocol: "tcp" | "udp",
      step: (label: string) => Promise<unknown>,
    
    
      unitName: string | null,
    
    
      verifyBound: () => Promise<boolean | null>,
    
    
      startupVerdict?: () => Promise<string | null>
    ): Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason: string; aborted?: boolean }> {
      const deadline = Date.now() + GAME_INSTALL_MAX_WAIT_MS;
      let probeFailures = 0;
      let lastProbeError = "";
      let lastUsedKb = -1;
      let firstRestarts = -1;
      let stalls = 0;
      let samples = 0;
    
    
    
      let lastCpuUsec = -1;
      const probe = async (): Promise<{ usedKb: number; logBytes: number; restarts: number; cpuUsec: number } | null> => {
        lastProbeError = "";
        try {
          return await withServerSsh(probeServer as any, async (conn) => {
            const r = await exec(
              conn,
              `used=$(df --output=used -k / 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | tr -d ' '); ` +
                `c=$(docker ps -q 2>/dev/null | head -1); ` +
                `lb=0; if [ -n "$c" ]; then lb=$(docker logs --since ${Math.round(GAME_INSTALL_SAMPLE_MS / 1000) + 10}s "$c" 2>&1 | wc -c); fi; ` +
                (unitName
                  ? `nr=$(systemctl show ${unitName} -p NRestarts --value 2>/dev/null); `
                  : `nr=""; `) +
                `case "${"$"}nr" in ''|*[!0-9]*) nr=-1;; esac; ` +
    
    
                `cu=-1; if [ -n "$c" ]; then id=$(docker inspect -f '{{.Id}}' "$c" 2>/dev/null); ` +
                `f=/sys/fs/cgroup/system.slice/docker-$id.scope/cpu.stat; ` +
                `[ -r "$f" ] && cu=$(awk '/^usage_usec/{print $2}' "$f"); fi; ` +
                `case "${"$"}cu" in ''|*[!0-9]*) cu=-1;; esac; ` +
                `echo "NXP:${"$"}{used:-0}:${"$"}{lb:-0}:${"$"}nr:${"$"}cu"`,
              QUICK_TIMEOUT_MS
            );
            const m = /NXP:(\d+):(\d+):(-?\d+)(?::(-?\d+))?/.exec(r.stdout);
            if (!m) {
              lastProbeError = "the probe printed no result";
              return null;
            }
            return { usedKb: Number(m[1]), logBytes: Number(m[2]), restarts: Number(m[3]), cpuUsec: m[4] !== undefined ? Number(m[4]) : -1 };
          });
        } catch (e) {
          lastProbeError = String((e as Error)?.message ?? e).slice(0, 200);
          return null;
        }
      };
    
      await step("Installing the game — this one is a large download…");
    
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        if (startupVerdict) {
          const why = await startupVerdict().catch(() => null);
          if (why) return { ok: false, reason: why, aborted: true };
        }
        const p = await probe();
        if (!p) {
    
    
    
    
          if (++probeFailures < 3) {
            await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 15_000));
            continue;
          }
          return {
            ok: false,
            reason: `Could not check whether the install was still running: ${probeFailures} consecutive guest reads failed (last: ${lastProbeError || "unknown error"}).`,
          };
        }
        probeFailures = 0;
        samples++;
        if (firstRestarts < 0) firstRestarts = p.restarts;
    
    
    
    
        if (gameUnitCrashLooping(firstRestarts, p.restarts)) {
          return {
            ok: false,
            reason:
              `The game container is crash-looping — its service restarted ${p.restarts - firstRestarts} times ` +
              `in ${samples} minute(s) of waiting (systemd NRestarts is now ${p.restarts}), so it was not still installing.`,
          };
        }
        if (lastUsedKb >= 0) {
          if (installProgressed({ usedKb: lastUsedKb, logBytes: 0, cpuUsec: lastCpuUsec }, p)) {
            stalls = 0;
          } else if (++stalls >= GAME_INSTALL_STALL_LIMIT) {
            return {
              ok: false,
              reason:
                `The install stopped making progress (${stalls} consecutive minutes with no disk growth, no container CPU work and no ` +
                `container output, at ${Math.round(p.usedKb / 1024)} MB used), so it was not simply still downloading.`,
            };
          }
        }
        lastUsedKb = p.usedKb;
        lastCpuUsec = p.cpuUsec;
    
    
        const c = await checkGamePortOpen(checkHost, checkPort, protocol, {
          timeoutMs: GAME_INSTALL_SAMPLE_MS,
          intervalMs: GAME_PORT_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS,
          verifyBound,
        });
        if (c.ok) {
          return { ok: true, reason: `still installing for a further ~${samples} min, then the port opened` };
        }
      }
      return {
        ok: false,
        reason: `It was still installing after ${Math.round(GAME_INSTALL_MAX_WAIT_MS / 60_000)} minutes, which is longer than this platform waits.`,
      };
    }
    
    async function provision(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
    
    
      await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "PROVISIONING", provisionError: null, provisioningStep: null } });
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const step = (label: string) =>
        db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { provisioningStep: label } }).catch(() => undefined);
    
      if (SANDBOX) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        const vmid = server.vmid ?? (await allocateSandboxVmid(server.id));
        const password = generatePassword();
        const ip = sandboxIp(vmid);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
        await step("Allocating resources…");
        await sleep(1800);
        await step("Configuring the network…");
        await sleep(1800);
        await step("Starting your server…");
        await sleep(1800);
        await step("Booting the image…");
        await sleep(2200);
        await step("Waiting for network…");
        await sleep(1600);
        if (server.gamePlanId) {
          await step("Starting the game server…");
          await sleep(1600);
          await step("Installing the game…");
          await sleep(3200);
        }
        await db.server.update({
          where: { id: server.id },
          data: { ipAddress: ip, sshPort: null, rootPassword: null, rootPasswordEnc: encryptSecret(password), status: "RUNNING" },
        });
        await notify(
          server.userId,
          `Server ${server.hostname} is ready`,
          `Sandbox server provisioned (no real VM was created). ssh root@${ip}`,
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
        );
    
        await sendReviewRequestForServer(server.id);
        return;
      }
    
      const { conn, row: nodeRow } = await nodeFor(server);
      const storage = server.plan.storageTier === "hdd"
        ? nodeRow?.storageHdd ?? "vmdata-hdd"
        : nodeRow?.storageNvme ?? "vmdata";
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const backupStorage = nodeRow?.isPrimary ? "backup" : (nodeRow?.storageHdd ?? "backup");
      const vmid = server.vmid ?? (await allocateFreshVmid(conn, backupStorage));
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const password = server.vmid ? (serverRootPassword(server) ?? generatePassword()) : generatePassword();
    
    
    
    
    
      let dedicated: { address: string; virtualMac: string; gateway: string } | null = null;
      const { getSettingBool: _settingBool } = await import("../lib/settings");
      const isGame = !!server.gamePlanId;
      const wantDedicated = isGame
        ? server.wantsDedicatedIp
        : server.wantsDedicatedIp || (await _settingBool("dedicated_ip_default", true));
      if (wantDedicated && (await dedicatedIpProviderConfigured(nodeRow?.id))) {
        try {
    
    
    
    
    
    
          dedicated = await allocateDedicatedIp(server.id, vmid, nodeRow?.id);
          if (!dedicated) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
            console.error(
              `[worker] dedicated IP requested for server ${server.id} (node ${nodeRow?.id}) but the pool had no FREE address — falling back to NAT. This customer paid for an IP they did not receive.`,
            );
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("[worker] dedicated IP allocation failed, falling back to NAT", e);
        }
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let inventory: NodeIpInventory | null = null;
      if (!dedicated && process.env.NXEON_IP_INVENTORY !== "off") {
        try {
          inventory = await nodeIpInventory(conn, {
            neighbors: nodeRow?.isPrimary ?? true ? localNeighbors : () => agentNeighbors(nodeRow as any),
            neighborSource: nodeRow?.isPrimary ?? true ? "local" : "node-agent",
          });
        } catch (e: any) {
          throw new Error(
            `Setup could not confirm a free private address for this server, so it stopped before creating anything.\n` +
              `Reading the VM configs on ${nodeRow?.name ?? conn.node} failed: ${String(e?.message ?? e).slice(0, 240)}`
          );
        }
        if (!inventory.neighbors) {
          console.warn(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: no ARP table for ${nodeRow?.name ?? conn.node} (node-agent < v4?) — IP check uses VM configs only`);
        }
      }
      const nodeHeld = inventory ? nodeHeldIps(inventory.entries, inventory.neighbors, vmid) : undefined;
    
    
      let ip = dedicated ? dedicated.address : (server.ipAddress ?? (await allocateIp(server.nodeId, server.id, { heldElsewhere: nodeHeld })));
      if (!dedicated && inventory) {
    
        const conflict = ipConflictReason(ip, vmid, inventory.entries, inventory.neighbors);
        if (conflict) {
          throw new ProvisionStopError(
            `This server's private network address is already in use by another machine, so setup stopped before using it.\n` +
              `${conflict} (server ${server.hostname}, VM ${vmid}, node ${nodeRow?.name ?? conn.node}). Nothing was changed on the other VM. ` +
              `Resolve the duplicate (an owner decision), then retry the provision.`
          );
        }
      }
      let sshPort = dedicated ? null : (server.sshPort ?? (await allocateSshPort(server.nodeId, server.id)));
    
      try {
        await db.server.update({
          where: { id: server.id },
          data: { vmid, ipAddress: ip, sshPort, rootPassword: null, rootPasswordEnc: encryptSecret(password) },
        });
      } catch (e: any) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        if (dedicated && e?.code === "P2002") {
          console.error(`[worker] dedicated IP ${dedicated.address} collided with a stale Server.ipAddress — self-healing and falling back to NAT`, e);
          await db.server.updateMany({ where: { ipAddress: dedicated.address }, data: { ipAddress: null, ipv6: null } });
          await releaseDedicatedIp(server.id).catch(() => {});
          dedicated = null;
          ip = server.ipAddress ?? (await allocateIp(server.nodeId, server.id, { heldElsewhere: nodeHeld }));
          sshPort = server.sshPort ?? (await allocateSshPort(server.nodeId, server.id));
          await db.server.update({
            where: { id: server.id },
            data: { vmid, ipAddress: ip, sshPort, rootPassword: null, rootPasswordEnc: encryptSecret(password) },
          });
        } else {
          throw e;
        }
      }
    
      const stockBaseVmid = server.osTemplate.baseVmid ?? server.osTemplate.vmid;
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const goldenVmid = goldenTemplateVmidFromEnv(process.env.NXEON_GOLDEN_GAME_TEMPLATE, server.osTemplate?.family ?? null);
      let goldenConfig: any = null;
      if (goldenVmid && isGame && stockBaseVmid === GOLDEN_SOURCE_VMID) {
        goldenConfig = await conn.get(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${goldenVmid}/config`).catch(() => null);
      }
      const cloneSource = chooseCloneSource({
        baseVmid: stockBaseVmid,
        isGame,
        goldenVmid,
        nodeConfig: goldenConfig,
        targetDiskGB: effectiveDiskGB(server.gamePlan?.diskGB ?? server.plan.diskGB, server.extraDiskGB),
      });
      const baseVmid = cloneSource.vmid;
      if (goldenVmid) console.log(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: clone source ${baseVmid} (${cloneSource.reason})`);
    
    
    
    
    
      let vmAlreadyExists = false;
      try {
        await conn.vmStatus(vmid);
        vmAlreadyExists = true;
      } catch {
        vmAlreadyExists = false;
      }
      if (vmAlreadyExists) {
        console.log(`[worker] vm ${vmid} already exists — skipping clone (idempotent retry)`);
      } else {
        await step("Allocating your server…");
    
    
    
    
    
        const linked = cloneSource.golden && linkedCloneAllowed({
          enabled: process.env.NXEON_GOLDEN_LINKED_CLONE === "1",
          templateDiskSpec: goldenConfig?.scsi0 ?? null,
          targetStorage: storage,
        });
        const cloneUpid = await conn.post(
          `/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${baseVmid}/clone`,
          linked ? { newid: vmid, name: server.hostname, full: 0 } : { newid: vmid, name: server.hostname, full: 1, storage },
        );
        await conn.waitForTask(cloneUpid, 600_000);
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      if (isGame) {
        const fam = server.osTemplate?.family ?? null;
        const cfg0 = ((server.gameConfig as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
        const toMint = adminSecretFieldsToMint(fam, server.osTemplate?.setupScript ?? null, cfg0);
    
    
        const identity = blankIdentityDefaults(fam, server.osTemplate?.setupScript ?? null, cfg0);
        if (toMint.length > 0 || Object.keys(identity).length > 0) {
          const next = { ...cfg0, ...identity };
          for (const k of toMint) next[k] = mintGameSecret();
          await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { gameConfig: next as any } });
          (server as any).gameConfig = next;
          (server as any).__mintedConfigKeys = toMint;
          if (toMint.length) console.log(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: minted per-server ${toMint.join(", ")} (catalog default was a shared placeholder)`);
          if (Object.keys(identity).length) console.log(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: defaulted blank ${Object.keys(identity).join(", ")} (a blank admin account name stops the game from starting)`);
        }
      }
      (server as any).__nxcloudSetup = await nxcloudSetupForServer(server);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const guestAlreadyBooted =
        vmAlreadyExists &&
        !!(server as any).telemetryTokenHash &&
        (await conn.vmStatus(vmid).then((st: { status?: string } | null) => st?.status === "running").catch(() => false));
      (server as any).__telemetryToken = guestAlreadyBooted ? null : await mintServerTelemetryToken(server.id);
    
      (server as any).__mcJarPin = await mcJarPinForSetup(server as any);
    
      (server as any).__terrariaPlan = await terrariaSetupPlan(server as any);
    
      await step("Configuring the network…");
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const snippetStorage = nodeRow?.isPrimary ? SNIPPET_STORAGE : (nodeRow?.storageHdd ?? SNIPPET_STORAGE);
      const snippetFilename = `nxeon-${vmid}.yaml`;
      let cicustom: string | undefined;
      if (nodeRow?.isPrimary ?? true) {
        const snippet = buildCloudInit(server, { golden: cloneSource.golden });
        try {
    
    
    
    
          await writeFile(`${SNIPPET_DIR}/${snippetFilename}`, snippet, { encoding: "utf8", mode: 0o600 });
        } catch (e: any) {
          throw new Error(
            `could not write the cloud-init snippet ${SNIPPET_DIR}/${snippetFilename} (${e?.message ?? e}). ` +
              `The VM would boot with no root password, no SSH key and no setup script, so provisioning ` +
              `stopped here rather than reporting a server that would not be usable.`
          );
        }
        cicustom = `vendor=${snippetStorage}:snippets/${snippetFilename}`;
      } else if (nodeRow) {
        const snippet = buildCloudInit(server, { golden: cloneSource.golden });
        try {
          await agentWriteSnippet(nodeRow, snippetStorage, snippetFilename, snippet);
          cicustom = `vendor=${snippetStorage}:snippets/${snippetFilename}`;
          console.log(`[worker] wrote a fresh per-VM cloud-init snippet for ${server.id} via node-agent on ${nodeRow.name}`);
        } catch (e: any) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          console.warn(
            `[worker] provisioning ${server.id} on non-primary node ${nodeRow.name} — node-agent snippet write ` +
              `failed (${e instanceof NodeAgentError ? e.message : String(e?.message ?? e)}), keeping the template's ` +
              `static vendor snippet (ssh_pwauth + guest-agent startup) instead; the game/app setup script, Claude ` +
              `Code, and other add-ons are skipped for this provision.`
          );
        }
      } else {
        console.warn(
          `[worker] provisioning ${server.id} with no resolved node row — keeping the template's static vendor ` +
            `snippet instead of a fresh per-VM one; the game/app setup script, Claude Code, and other add-ons are ` +
            `skipped for this provision.`
        );
      }
    
      const keys = await db.sshKey.findMany({ where: { userId: server.userId } });
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      let managementPublicKey: string | undefined;
      let managementKeyEnc: string | null = null;
    
    
    
    
    
    
      if (shouldReuseManagementKey(server) && server.sshManagementKeyEnc && server.sshManagementPublicKey) {
        managementPublicKey = server.sshManagementPublicKey;
        managementKeyEnc = server.sshManagementKeyEnc;
      } else {
        try {
          const managementKeypair = generateManagementKeypair(`nxeon-mgmt-${server.id}`);
          managementKeyEnc = encryptSecret(managementKeypair.privateKeyOpenSsh);
          await db.server.update({
            where: { id: server.id },
            data: {
              sshManagementKeyEnc: managementKeyEnc,
              sshManagementPublicKey: managementKeypair.publicKeyLine,
            },
          });
          managementPublicKey = managementKeypair.publicKeyLine;
        } catch (e) {
          managementKeyEnc = null;
          console.error(`[worker] management key generation failed for ${server.id} — continuing without one`, e);
        }
      }
      const allPublicKeys = [...keys.map((k) => k.publicKey.trim()), ...(managementPublicKey ? [managementPublicKey] : [])];
      const sshkeys = encodeURIComponent(allPublicKeys.join("\n"));
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const effectiveCores = server.gamePlan?.cpuCores ?? server.plan.cpuCores;
      const effectiveRamMB = server.gamePlan?.ramMB ?? server.plan.ramMB;
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const effectiveBaseDiskGB = server.gamePlan?.diskGB ?? server.plan.diskGB;
      const cfg: Record<string, any> = {
        cores: effectiveCores,
        memory: effectiveRamMB,
        balloon: balloonForVm(effectiveRamMB),
        ciuser: "root",
        cipassword: password,
        sshkeys,
        nameserver: "1.1.1.1 8.8.8.8",
        onboot: 1,
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        ...(cicustom ? { cicustom } : {}),
        tags: `${process.env.NXEON_TAG ?? "nxeon"};user-${server.userId.slice(0, 8)}`,
      };
      if (dedicated && dedicated.virtualMac) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        cfg.net0 = `virtio=${dedicated.virtualMac},bridge=vmbr0`;
        cfg.ipconfig0 = `ip=${dedicated.address}/32,gw=${dedicated.gateway}`;
      } else if (dedicated) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        cfg.net0 = `virtio,bridge=vmbr0`;
        cfg.ipconfig0 = `ip=${dedicated.address}/32,gw=${dedicated.gateway}`;
      } else {
    
        cfg.ipconfig0 = `ip=${ip}/24,gw=${gatewayIp(nodeRow?.subnetPrefix)}`;
      }
      await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${vmid}/config`, cfg);
    
    
    
      await conn.put(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${vmid}/resize`, {
        disk: "scsi0",
        size: `${effectiveDiskGB(effectiveBaseDiskGB, server.extraDiskGB)}G`,
      });
    
    
    
    
    
      let natError: Error | null = null;
      if (!SANDBOX && !dedicated) natError = await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`provision ${server.hostname}`);
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const alreadyRunning = await conn
        .vmStatus(vmid)
        .then((s: { status?: string } | null) => s?.status === "running")
        .catch(() => false);
      if (!alreadyRunning) {
        await step("Starting your server…");
        const startUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${vmid}/status/start`, {});
        try {
          await conn.waitForTask(startUpid);
        } catch (e) {
          if (!/already running/i.test(String((e as { message?: unknown })?.message ?? ""))) throw e;
        }
      }
    
    
    
      if (!SANDBOX) await step("Booting the operating system…");
      const booted = SANDBOX ? true : await waitForGuestAgent(conn, conn.node, vmid);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      if (!SANDBOX) {
    
    
    
    
        const sshHost = dedicated ? dedicated.address : (server.node?.publicIp ?? process.env.PUBLIC_IP);
        const sshCheckPort = dedicated ? 22 : (sshPort ?? 22);
        await step("Waiting for network access…");
        const ssh = await checkSshReachable(sshHost!, sshCheckPort);
        if (!ssh.ok) {
          throw new Error(
            `SSH did not respond on ${sshHost}:${sshCheckPort} after ~${Math.round(ssh.elapsedMs / 1000)}s ` +
              `(${ssh.attempts} attempt(s); last error: ${ssh.error}). The VM started, but nothing answered SSH, ` +
              `so this server cannot be handed to the customer as ready.`
          );
        }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        const guestSrv = {
          id: server.id,
          rootPassword: password,
          rootPasswordEnc: null,
          sshManagementKeyEnc: managementKeyEnc,
          sshPort: dedicated ? 22 : sshPort,
          dedicatedIp: dedicated ? { address: dedicated.address } : null,
          node: server.node,
        };
        const withGuest: WithGuest = withGuestFor(guestSrv as any);
        const guestChecks = process.env.NXEON_PROVISION_GUEST_CHECKS !== "0";
        if (guestChecks) {
          await step("Checking access to your server…");
          const access = await ensureGuestAccess({
            withGuest,
            reapplyCredentials: () => applyPasswordToGuest(conn, vmid, password),
          });
          if (!access.ok) {
            const msg =
              `Setup could not log in to the new server to finish it, so it was not handed over as ready.\n` +
              `${access.reason} (VM ${vmid}, ${sshHost}:${sshCheckPort}).`;
    
            throw access.authFailed && /DIFFERENT machine/.test(access.reason) ? new ProvisionStopError(msg) : new Error(msg);
          }
          if (access.repaired) {
            console.warn(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: the guest rejected its stored credentials; re-applied the root password via the guest agent and got in`);
          }
          const vmCfg = await conn.get(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${vmid}/config`).catch(() => null);
          const identity = await verifyGuestIdentity(withGuest, { uuid: smbiosUuid(vmCfg?.smbios1), hostname: server.hostname });
          if (!identity.ok) {
            throw new ProvisionStopError(
              `The network address assigned to this server reaches a different machine, so setup stopped before running anything on it.\n` +
                `${identity.reason} (VM ${vmid}, ${sshHost}:${sshCheckPort}, private ${ip}). Check the node for a duplicate private IP; ` +
                `the other machine was not touched.`
            );
          }
          if (identity.how === "unverified") console.warn(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: guest identity could not be read (no SMBIOS UUID / hostname) — continuing`);
    
    
          await installManagementKey(withGuest, managementPublicKey).catch((e) =>
            console.warn(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: could not confirm the management key in the guest: ${String(e?.message ?? e).slice(0, 160)}`)
          );
        }
    
        const game = gamePortForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike);
        if (game) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
          await step("Starting the game server…");
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          let checkHost = ip;
          let checkPort = game.port;
          if (!SANDBOX && !dedicated && !nodeRow?.isPrimary) {
    
    
    
            const earlyFam = gameFamilyForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike);
            if (earlyFam && contiguousForwardPorts(earlyFam)) {
              try {
                await ensureGameForwards(server.nodeId, server.id, earlyFam);
              } catch (e) {
                console.error(`[worker] block forward allocation failed for ${server.hostname}`, e);
              }
            }
            const existing = await db.portForward.findFirst({
              where: { serverId: server.id, guestPort: game.port, protocol: game.protocol },
              select: { publicPort: true },
            });
            checkPort = existing?.publicPort ?? (await allocateGameForward(server.nodeId, server.id, game.port, game.protocol));
            const natErr = await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`provision ${server.hostname} (game port)`);
            if (natErr) {
              throw new Error(
                `could not sync the game port forward on ${nodeRow?.name ?? "the target node"} for verification: ${natErr}`
              );
            }
            checkHost = server.node?.publicIp ?? process.env.PUBLIC_IP ?? ip;
          }
    
    
    
          const probeServer = guestSrv;
    
    
          const verifyBound = () => gamePortBoundInGuest(probeServer, game.port, game.protocol);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          const startupFam = gameFamilyForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike);
          const startupUnit = controlSpec(startupFam ?? "")?.serviceHints[0] ?? null;
          const startupContainer = dockerContainerNameFrom(server.osTemplate?.setupScript ?? "");
          const startupSecrets = [...secretValuesFromConfig((server as any).gameConfig ?? null), password];
          const freshGameScript = (): string | null => {
            try {
              return buildGameSetupScript({ ...server, gameConfig: (server as any).gameConfig, __mcJarPin: (server as any).__mcJarPin } as any);
            } catch (e: any) {
              console.error(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: could not rebuild the game setup for a re-run: ${String(e?.message ?? e).slice(0, 200)}`);
              return null;
            }
          };
          let startupBaseline = -1;
          let repairsLeft = 1;
          let fatalStartup: string | null = null;
    
    
          let fatalNoRetry = false;
          const startupVerdict = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
            if (!guestChecks || !startupUnit || !startupContainer) return null;
            let sample: StartupSample | null = null;
            try {
              sample = await sampleStartup(withGuest, startupUnit, startupContainer);
            } catch {
              return null; // unknown is never a verdict
            }
            if (!sample) return null;
            if (startupBaseline < 0) startupBaseline = sample.restarts;
            const v = classifyStartup(startupBaseline, sample);
            if (v.kind === "fatal") {
              fatalNoRetry = v.recognised;
              fatalStartup =
                `The game failed to start on the new server (it stops right after starting), so it was not handed over as ready.\n` +
                `${v.reason}. The game's own last lines:\n${formatGuestLines(v.lines, startupSecrets)}`;
              return fatalStartup;
            }
            if (v.kind === "repair") {
              const script = freshGameScript();
              if (repairsLeft > 0 && script) {
                repairsLeft--;
                const r = await relaunchGameSetup(withGuest, script).catch((e) => ({ launched: false, alreadyRunning: false, detail: String(e?.message ?? e) }));
                console.warn(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: ${v.reason} — re-launched the game setup (${r.launched ? "started" : r.alreadyRunning ? "already running" : `not started: ${r.detail}`})`);
                startupBaseline = -1;
                await step("Retrying the game download…");
                return null;
              }
              return (
                `The game could not be installed on the new server, so it was not handed over as ready.\n` +
                `${v.reason}, and re-running the setup did not fix it. Last setup output:\n${formatGuestLines(v.lines, startupSecrets)}`
              );
            }
            return null;
          };
    
    
    
    
          if (vmAlreadyExists && alreadyRunning && guestChecks) {
            const script = freshGameScript();
            if (script) {
              await step("Re-applying the game setup…");
              const r = await relaunchGameSetup(withGuest, script).catch((e) => ({ launched: false, alreadyRunning: false, detail: String(e?.message ?? e) }));
              console.log(`[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: retry — re-applied the game setup (${r.launched ? "started" : r.alreadyRunning ? "already running" : `not started: ${r.detail}`})`);
            }
          }
          await startupVerdict(); // baseline restart count before the wait starts
          if (fatalStartup) throw fatalNoRetry ? new ProvisionStopError(fatalStartup) : new Error(fatalStartup);
    
          const gameCheck = await checkGamePortOpen(checkHost, checkPort, game.protocol, {
            verifyBound,
    
            shouldAbort: async (attempt) => (attempt % 3 === 0 ? startupVerdict() : null),
          });
          if (!gameCheck.ok && gameCheck.aborted) {
            throw fatalStartup && fatalNoRetry ? new ProvisionStopError(gameCheck.error) : new Error(gameCheck.error);
          }
          if (!gameCheck.ok) {
    
    
    
    
            const still = await waitOutGameInstall(
              probeServer,
              checkHost,
              checkPort,
              game.protocol,
              step,
              controlSpec(gameFamilyForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike) ?? "")?.serviceHints[0] ?? null,
              verifyBound,
              startupVerdict
            );
            if (!still.ok && still.aborted) {
              throw fatalStartup && fatalNoRetry ? new ProvisionStopError(still.reason) : new Error(still.reason);
            }
            if (!still.ok) {
    
    
              let bedrockHint = "";
              if (startupFam === "bedrock" && guestChecks) {
                try {
                  const lr = await withGuest((sh) => sh.exec(`docker logs ${startupContainer ?? "bedrock"} 2>&1 | tail -n 120`, QUICK_TIMEOUT_MS));
                  const h = bedrockStartupHint(lr.stdout);
                  if (h) bedrockHint = ` Likely cause: ${h}`;
                } catch {
                  
                }
              }
              throw new Error(
                `game port ${game.port}/${game.protocol} never opened on ${checkHost}:${checkPort} after ` +
                  `~${Math.round(gameCheck.elapsedMs / 1000)}s (${gameCheck.attempts} attempt(s); last error: ` +
                  `${gameCheck.error}). SSH is up, but the game server itself never started listening, so this ` +
                  `server cannot be handed to the customer as ready. ${still.reason}${bedrockHint}`
              );
            }
            console.log(`[worker] ${server.hostname}: game port opened after the extended install wait (${still.reason})`);
          }
    
    
    
    
          {
            const a2sFam = gameFamilyForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike);
            if (!SANDBOX && guestChecks && a2sFam && A2S_READINESS_FAMILIES.has(a2sFam)) {
              const joinForA2s = joinPortFor(a2sFam);
              await step("Waiting for the game to load its map…");
              const a2s = await checkA2sAnswers(
                joinForA2s.port,
                async (script) => {
                  const r = await withGuest((sh) => sh.exec(script, QUICK_TIMEOUT_MS));
                  return r.stdout;
                },
                { shouldAbort: async (attempt) => (attempt % 3 === 0 ? startupVerdict() : null) }
              );
              if (!a2s.ok) {
                throw new Error(
                  `${a2sFam} is not answering game queries on ${joinForA2s.port}/${joinForA2s.protocol} after ` +
                    `~${Math.round(a2s.elapsedMs / 1000)}s (${a2s.attempts} attempt(s)): ${a2s.error}. ` +
                    `The port is open but the game itself is not serving, so this server cannot be handed over as ready.`
                );
              }
            }
          }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          const joinFam = gameFamilyForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike);
          const join = joinFam ? joinPortFor(joinFam) : null;
          const checkPortIsAdminOnly = !!join && (join.port !== game.port || join.protocol !== game.protocol);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          if (!SANDBOX && !dedicated && joinFam) {
    
    
    
            try {
              await ensureGameForwards(server.nodeId, server.id, joinFam);
            } catch (e) {
    
    
              console.error(`[worker] auto-forward failed for ${server.hostname}`, e);
            }
            const natErr = await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`provision ${server.hostname} (join ports)`);
            if (natErr) console.error(`[worker] join port forward sync failed for ${server.hostname}`, natErr);
          }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          if (!SANDBOX && !dedicated && checkPortIsAdminOnly) {
            try {
              const removed = await db.portForward.deleteMany({
                where: { serverId: server.id, guestPort: game.port, protocol: game.protocol },
              });
              if (removed.count > 0) {
                const natErr = await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`provision ${server.hostname} (retract admin port)`);
                if (natErr) console.error(`[worker] admin port retraction sync failed for ${server.hostname}`, natErr);
              }
            } catch (e) {
              console.error(`[worker] could not retract the admin-port forward for ${server.hostname}`, e);
            }
          }
    
    
    
          if (server.subdomain) {
            await db.job.create({ data: { type: "SYNC_GAME_DNS", serverId: server.id } });
          }
        } else if (server.gamePlanId) {
    
    
    
    
    
          console.error(
            `[worker] provision ${server.hostname}: game order (gamePlanId=${server.gamePlanId}, ` +
              `osTemplate.family=${server.osTemplate?.family ?? "?"}) has no GAME_PORT entry — skipped the game-port check`
          );
        }
      }
    
    
    
    
    
      const readiness = !SANDBOX && gamePortForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike)
        ? await runProvisionReadiness(server.id)
        : null;
    
      const runningServer = await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "RUNNING", provisioningStep: null } });
    
    
      try {
        const { sendServerDeployedEmail } = await import("../lib/server-deployed-email");
        await sendServerDeployedEmail(server, (server as any).user);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[worker] deployment-confirmation email failed (non-fatal)", e);
      }
    
    
    
    
      if (runningServer.partnerSource === "kodely") {
        try {
          const { notifyPartner } = await import("../lib/partner");
          await notifyPartner("vps.provisioned", {
            userId: runningServer.userId,
            serverId: runningServer.id,
            hostname: runningServer.hostname,
            ipAddress: runningServer.ipAddress,
          });
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("[worker] partner notify failed (non-fatal)", e);
        }
      }
    
      const isImage = server.osTemplate.category !== "PLAIN_OS";
      const sshLine = dedicated
        ? `ssh root@${dedicated.address}`
        : `ssh root@${server.node?.publicIp ?? process.env.PUBLIC_IP} -p ${sshPort}`;
    
      const caveats: string[] = [];
      if (natError) {
        caveats.push(
          `Port forwarding could NOT be confirmed for this server, so the SSH command above may not connect yet — ` +
            `we are not going to pretend otherwise. Support has been alerted (${natError.message.slice(0, 200)}).`
        );
      }
      if (readiness && !readiness.ok) {
        caveats.push(
          "Your server is up, but our post-setup check found something that needs a look (" +
            readiness.checks.filter((c) => c.status === "fail").map((c) => c.label.toLowerCase()).join(", ") +
            "). Our team has been alerted and will follow up."
        );
      }
      if (!booted) {
        caveats.push(
          "The hypervisor started the VM, but its guest agent has not answered yet, so we cannot confirm the " +
            "operating system finished booting. Give it a few more minutes; if the SSH command still fails, open a ticket."
        );
      }
      const body = [
        isImage ? `${server.osTemplate.name} is installing in the background (a few minutes). ${sshLine}` : sshLine,
        ...caveats,
      ].join(" — ");
      await notify(
        server.userId,
    
        caveats.length ? `Server ${server.hostname} is up, with caveats` : `Server ${server.hostname} is ready`,
        body,
        `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
      );
    
      await sendReviewRequestForServer(server.id);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      try {
        const gameFamily = gameFamilyForServer(server as unknown as ServerFamilyLike);
        const schema = gameFamily ? schemaFor(gameFamily) : null;
        const gameConfig = (server.gameConfig as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? {};
        const minted: string[] = (server as any).__mintedConfigKeys ?? [];
        const hasNonDefaultConfig = !!schema && schema.fields.some((f) => {
          if (minted.includes(f.key)) return false; // already baked into the setup script
          const val = gameConfig[f.key];
          return val !== undefined && val !== null && String(val) !== String(f.default);
        });
    
    
        const applyAtProvision = !!gameFamily && !!applierSpec(gameFamily)?.applyAtProvision;
        if (schema && ((server.gameVariant && server.gameVariant !== schema.defaultVariant) || hasNonDefaultConfig || applyAtProvision)) {
          await db.job.create({ data: { type: "GAME_CONFIG", serverId: server.id } });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`[worker] could not auto-queue GAME_CONFIG for ${server.hostname}'s checkout-time variant`, e);
      }
    }
    
    async function powerAction(job: Job, action: "start" | "stop" | "reboot") {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      if (SANDBOX) {
        await db.server.update({
          where: { id: server.id },
          data: { status: action === "stop" ? "STOPPED" : "RUNNING" },
        });
        return;
      }
      const { conn } = await nodeFor(server);
      const upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/${action}`, {});
      await conn.waitForTask(upid);
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: server.id },
        data: { status: action === "stop" ? "STOPPED" : "RUNNING" },
      });
    }
    
    
    async function destroyVm(conn: any, vmid: number): Promise<DestroyOutcome> {
      const host: VmDestroyHost = {
        probe: async (id) => {
          try {
            await conn.vmStatus(id);
            return "present";
          } catch (e) {
    
    
    
            return isVmAbsentError(e, id) ? "absent" : "unknown";
          }
        },
        stop: async (id) => {
          const stopUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${id}/status/stop`, {});
          await conn.waitForTask(stopUpid);
        },
        destroy: async (id) => {
          const delUpid = await conn.del(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${id}`, {
            "destroy-unreferenced-disks": 1,
            purge: 1,
          });
          await conn.waitForTask(delUpid, 600_000);
        },
      };
      return destroyVmIdempotent(host, vmid);
    }
    
    
    function classifyDeleteReason(server: { autoRenew: boolean; suspendedNote: string | null; expiresAt: Date | null }): string {
      if (server.suspendedNote === "ABUSE" || server.suspendedNote === "ADMIN") return "REMOVED";
      if (server.expiresAt && server.expiresAt <= new Date()) {
        return server.autoRenew ? "EXPIRED" : "CANCELLED";
      }
      return "DELETED";
    }
    
    async function archiveServerBeforeDelete(
      server: Server & { plan: any; gamePlan: any; node: any; user: any },
    ): Promise<void> {
      const spendByServerId = await resolveServerLifetimeSpendCents([
        {
          id: server.id,
          hostname: server.hostname,
          previousHostnames: server.previousHostnames,
          userId: server.userId,
          createdAt: server.createdAt,
        },
      ]);
      await db.serverArchive.create({
        data: {
          originalId: server.id,
          hostname: server.hostname,
          userId: server.userId,
          userEmail: server.user?.email ?? "",
          isGameServer: !!server.gamePlan,
          gameFamily: server.gamePlan?.gameFamily ?? null,
          serviceName: server.gamePlan?.name ?? server.plan?.name ?? "—",
          nodeName: server.node?.name ?? null,
          region: server.node?.region ?? null,
          vmid: server.vmid,
          totalSpentCents: spendByServerId.get(server.id) ?? 0,
          provisionedAt: server.createdAt,
          expiresAt: server.expiresAt,
          reason: classifyDeleteReason(server),
        },
      });
    }
    
    async function remove(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      const { conn, row: nodeRow } = await nodeFor(server);
    
    
    
    
      await teardownMoveLeftoversForServer(server.id).catch((e) => console.error("[worker] move-leftover teardown failed", e));
      if (!SANDBOX && server.vmid) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        try {
          await destroyVm(conn, server.vmid);
        } catch (e: any) {
          if (!isVmAbsentError(e, server.vmid)) throw e;
        }
    
    
    
    
    
        if (nodeRow?.isPrimary ?? true) {
          await unlink(`${SNIPPET_DIR}/nxeon-${server.vmid}.yaml`).catch(() => {});
        } else if (nodeRow) {
          const storage = nodeRow.storageHdd ?? SNIPPET_STORAGE;
          await agentDeleteSnippet(nodeRow, storage, `nxeon-${server.vmid}.yaml`).catch(() => {});
        }
      }
    
      if (!SANDBOX) await releaseDedicatedIp(server.id).catch((e) => console.error("[worker] release dedicated IP failed", e));
    
    
    
    
      const gslteamid = (server.gameConfig as Record<string, unknown> | null)?._SRCDS_TOKEN_STEAMID;
      if (!SANDBOX && typeof gslteamid === "string") {
        await deleteGameServerAccount(gslteamid).catch((e) => console.error("[worker] Steam GSLT account release failed", e));
      }
    
    
    
      if (!SANDBOX && server.subdomain) await deleteMinecraftSubdomainDns(server.subdomain).catch((e) => console.error("[worker] delete game subdomain DNS failed", e));
    
    
    
    
    
      const orphanVolumes = await db.volume.findMany({ where: { serverId: server.id } });
      if (orphanVolumes.length > 0) {
        await db.volume.deleteMany({ where: { serverId: server.id } });
        for (const v of orphanVolumes) {
          await notify(
            v.userId,
            `Volume "${v.name}" was removed`,
            `${v.sizeGB} GB block storage was deleted along with server ${server.hostname}. Billing for it has stopped.`,
            "/dashboard/volumes",
          ).catch(() => {});
        }
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      await archiveServerBeforeDelete(server).catch((e) => console.error("[worker] archive server failed", e));
    
    
    
      await db.job.updateMany({ where: { serverId: server.id }, data: { serverId: null } });
      await db.server.delete({ where: { id: server.id } });
    
    
    
      if (!SANDBOX) await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`delete ${server.hostname}`);
    }
    
    async function reinstall(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      if (SANDBOX) {
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "RUNNING" } });
        await notify(
          server.userId,
          `Server ${server.hostname} reinstalled (sandbox)`,
          "The sandbox server was reinstalled (no real VM was touched).",
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
        );
        return;
      }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      const pinnedVolumes = await db.volume.findMany({
        where: { serverId: server.id },
      });
    
      const realPinned = pinnedVolumes.filter(
        (v) => !(typeof v.diskName === "string" && v.diskName.startsWith("sandbox:"))
      );
      if (realPinned.length > 0) {
        const names = realPinned.map((v) => `"${v.name}"`).join(", ");
        throw new Error(
          `Remove your data volume(s) before reinstalling to protect their data: ${names}. ` +
            `Reinstalling purges every disk this server owns — including detached volumes ` +
            `that are still assigned to it — and that cannot be undone.`
        );
      }
    
      const { conn } = await nodeFor(server);
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: server.id },
        data: { status: "REINSTALLING", rootPassword: null, rootPasswordEnc: null },
      });
      if (server.vmid) await destroyVm(conn, server.vmid);
      await provision(job);
    }
    
    async function suspend(job: Job, unsuspend: boolean) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      if (SANDBOX) {
        if (unsuspend) {
          await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "RUNNING", suspendedNote: null, suspendReason: null } });
        } else {
          await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "SUSPENDED" } });
        }
        return;
      }
      const { conn, row: nodeRow } = await nodeFor(server);
      if (unsuspend) {
    
    
        if (server.vmid) {
          const { getSettingBool: _sb } = await import("../lib/settings");
          const isGameSrv = !!server.gamePlanId;
          const wantDedicated = isGameSrv
            ? server.wantsDedicatedIp
            : server.wantsDedicatedIp || (await _sb("dedicated_ip_default", true));
          const alreadyHas = await db.dedicatedIp.findFirst({ where: { serverId: server.id } });
          let reconfigured = alreadyHas ? true : false;
          if (wantDedicated && (await dedicatedIpProviderConfigured(nodeRow?.id)) && !alreadyHas) {
            try {
              const d = await allocateDedicatedIp(server.id, server.vmid, nodeRow?.id);
              if (d) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
                await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/config`, {
                  net0: d.virtualMac ? `virtio=${d.virtualMac},bridge=vmbr0` : `virtio,bridge=vmbr0`,
                  ipconfig0: `ip=${d.address}/32,gw=${d.gateway}`,
                });
                await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { ipAddress: d.address, sshPort: null } });
                reconfigured = true;
              }
            } catch (e) {
              console.error("[worker] re-allocate dedicated IP on unsuspend failed; falling back to NAT", e);
            }
          }
    
    
    
    
          if (!reconfigured) {
            try {
              const ip = server.ipAddress ?? (await allocateIp(server.nodeId, server.id));
              const sshPort = server.sshPort ?? (await allocateSshPort(server.nodeId, server.id));
              await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/config`, {
                net0: `virtio,bridge=${nodeRow?.bridge ?? "vmbr1"}`,
                ipconfig0: `ip=${ip}/24,gw=${gatewayIp(nodeRow?.subnetPrefix)}`,
              });
              await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { ipAddress: ip, sshPort } });
            } catch (e) {
              console.error("[worker] NAT fallback on unsuspend failed", e);
            }
          }
        }
    
        if (server.vmid) await pinVmMemoryBestEffort(conn, server.vmid, `unsuspend ${server.hostname}`);
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "STOPPED", suspendedNote: null, suspendReason: null } });
    
    
        if (!SANDBOX) await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`unsuspend ${server.hostname}`);
        const upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/start`, {});
        await conn.waitForTask(upid);
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "RUNNING" } });
    
    
        if (!SANDBOX && server.subdomain) await db.job.create({ data: { type: "SYNC_GAME_DNS", serverId: server.id } });
      } else {
        try {
          const upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/stop`, {});
          await conn.waitForTask(upid);
        } catch {  }
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { status: "SUSPENDED" } });
    
    
        if (!SANDBOX && server.subdomain) await db.job.create({ data: { type: "SYNC_GAME_DNS", serverId: server.id } });
        if (!SANDBOX) await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`suspend ${server.hostname}`);
    
    
    
    
    
    
        try {
          const had = await db.dedicatedIp.findFirst({ where: { serverId: server.id } });
    
    
    
          if (had) await releaseDedicatedIp(server.id);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("[worker] release dedicated IP on suspend failed", e);
        }
      }
    }
    
    async function notify(userId: string, title: string, body?: string, href?: string) {
      try {
        await db.notification.create({ data: { userId, title, body, href } });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[worker] notify failed", e);
      }
    }
    
    
    async function notifyProvisionFailed(
      server: { id: string; userId: string; hostname: string },
      error: string,
    ): Promise<void> {
    
    
      const reason = (error.split("\n")[0] || error).trim().slice(0, 300);
      try {
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { provisionError: reason } });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[worker] persist provisionError failed", e);
      }
    
    
    
    
      try {
        await db.nxCloudInstance.updateMany({
          where: { serverId: server.id, status: { notIn: ["DELETING", "ACTIVE"] } },
          data: { status: "FAILED", error: reason },
        });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[worker] mark nxcloud instance failed", e);
      }
      await notify(
        server.userId,
        `We couldn't set up ${server.hostname}`,
        `Provisioning failed and our team has been notified. You can retry it or contact support from the server page.`,
        `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`,
      );
      try {
        const { sendMail, emails } = await import("../lib/mailer");
        const user = await db.user.findUnique({ where: { id: server.userId }, select: { email: true } });
        if (user?.email) {
          const tpl = emails.provisionFailed({ hostname: server.hostname, reason });
          await sendMail({
            to: user.email,
            subject: tpl.subject,
            html: tpl.html,
            template: tpl.template,
            userId: server.userId,
    
    
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[worker] provision-failure email failed", e);
      }
    }
    
    async function resize(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
    
    
    
      const payload = (job.payload ?? {}) as { planId?: string; gamePlanId?: string };
      const newPlan = await db.plan.findUnique({ where: { id: payload.planId ?? "" } });
      if (!newPlan) throw new Error("resize: target plan not found");
      if (newPlan.diskGB < server.plan.diskGB) throw new Error("resize: disk can only grow");
      const newGamePlan = payload.gamePlanId
        ? await db.gamePlan.findUnique({ where: { id: payload.gamePlanId } })
        : null;
      if (payload.gamePlanId && !newGamePlan) throw new Error("resize: target game tier not found");
    
    
    
    
    
      const planPatch = newGamePlan
        ? { planId: newPlan.id, gamePlanId: newGamePlan.id, grandfatheredRenewalCents: newGamePlan.priceCents }
        : { planId: newPlan.id, grandfatheredRenewalCents: newPlan.priceCents };
      const resizedToName = newGamePlan?.name ?? newPlan.name;
    
    
    
    
    
      const newTotalDiskGB = effectiveDiskGB(newPlan.diskGB, server.extraDiskGB);
      const currentTotalDiskGB = effectiveDiskGB(server.plan.diskGB, server.extraDiskGB);
      const diskLine =
        server.extraDiskGB > 0
          ? `${newTotalDiskGB} GB disk (${newPlan.diskGB} GB plan + ${server.extraDiskGB} GB added storage)`
          : `${newPlan.diskGB} GB disk`;
    
      if (SANDBOX) {
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: planPatch });
        await notify(server.userId, `Server ${server.hostname} resized to ${resizedToName}`,
          `${newPlan.cpuCores} vCPU / ${newPlan.ramMB / 1024} GB RAM / ${diskLine}`,
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`);
        return;
      }
    
      const { conn } = await nodeFor(server);
      const wasRunning = server.status === "RUNNING";
      await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/config`, {
        cores: newPlan.cpuCores,
        memory: newPlan.ramMB,
        balloon: balloonForVm(newPlan.ramMB),
      });
      if (newTotalDiskGB > currentTotalDiskGB) {
        await conn.put(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/resize`, {
          disk: "scsi0",
          size: `${newTotalDiskGB}G`,
        });
      }
      await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: planPatch });
    
      if (wasRunning) {
        try {
          const stopUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/shutdown`, { timeout: 60 });
          await conn.waitForTask(stopUpid, 120_000);
        } catch {
          const stopUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/stop`, {});
          await conn.waitForTask(stopUpid);
        }
        const startUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/start`, {});
        await conn.waitForTask(startUpid);
      }
      await notify(server.userId, `Server ${server.hostname} resized to ${resizedToName}`,
        `${newPlan.cpuCores} vCPU / ${newPlan.ramMB / 1024} GB RAM / ${diskLine}`,
        `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`);
    }
    
    
    async function assignDedicatedIp(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      const existing = await db.dedicatedIp.findUnique({ where: { serverId: server.id } });
      if (existing) {
    
    
    
        return;
      }
    
      if (SANDBOX) {
    
    
    
    
    
        const address = sandboxIp(server.vmid ?? 0);
        const sandboxNodeId = server.nodeId ?? (await db.node.findFirst({ where: { isPrimary: true }, select: { id: true } }))?.id;
        if (!sandboxNodeId) throw new Error("assignDedicatedIp (sandbox): no node available to attribute the synthetic IP to");
        await db.$transaction([
          db.dedicatedIp.create({
            data: { address, ovhBlock: `${address}/32`, nodeId: sandboxNodeId, status: "ASSIGNED", serverId: server.id, purpose: "CUSTOMER" },
          }),
          db.server.update({
            where: { id: server.id },
            data: { ipAddress: address, sshPort: null, wantsDedicatedIp: true },
          }),
        ]);
        await notify(server.userId, `Dedicated IP added to ${server.hostname} (sandbox)`,
          `Your server now connects on ${address} (no real network was touched).`,
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`);
        return;
      }
    
      if (!server.vmid) throw new Error("assign dedicated ip: server has no VM yet");
      const d = await allocateDedicatedIp(server.id, server.vmid, server.nodeId ?? undefined);
      if (!d) throw new Error("assign dedicated ip: no free IP available in this server's node pool (or its provider isn't configured)");
    
      const { conn } = await nodeFor(server);
      const wasRunning = server.status === "RUNNING";
      await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/config`, {
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        net0: d.virtualMac ? `virtio=${d.virtualMac},bridge=vmbr0` : `virtio,bridge=vmbr0`,
        ipconfig0: `ip=${d.address}/32,gw=${d.gateway}`,
      });
      await db.server.update({
        where: { id: server.id },
        data: { ipAddress: d.address, sshPort: null, wantsDedicatedIp: true },
      });
    
      await pinVmMemoryBestEffort(conn, server.vmid, `assign dedicated ip ${server.hostname}`);
      if (wasRunning) {
        try {
          const stopUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/shutdown`, { timeout: 60 });
          await conn.waitForTask(stopUpid, 120_000);
        } catch {
          const stopUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/stop`, {});
          await conn.waitForTask(stopUpid);
        }
        const startUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/start`, {});
        await conn.waitForTask(startUpid);
      }
    
    
      await syncPortForwardsBestEffort(`assign dedicated ip ${server.hostname}`);
    
    
      if (server.subdomain) await db.job.create({ data: { type: "SYNC_GAME_DNS", serverId: server.id } });
      await notify(server.userId, `Dedicated IP added to ${server.hostname}`,
        `Your server now connects on ${d.address}.`,
        `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`);
    }
    
    async function snapshotOp(job: Job, op: "create" | "delete" | "rollback") {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      const payload = (job.payload ?? {}) as { name?: string };
      const name = (payload.name ?? "").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]/g, "").slice(0, 40);
      if (!name) throw new Error("snapshot: invalid name");
      if (SANDBOX) {
        await notify(
          server.userId,
          `Snapshot ${op} (sandbox): ${name}`,
          `Sandbox snapshot ${op} completed (no real VM was touched).`,
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
        );
        return;
      }
      const { conn } = await nodeFor(server);
      let upid: string;
      if (op === "create") {
        upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/snapshot`, { snapname: name });
      } else if (op === "delete") {
        upid = await conn.del(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/snapshot/${name}`);
      } else {
        upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/snapshot/${name}/rollback`, {});
      }
      await conn.waitForTask(upid, 600_000);
      if (op === "rollback") {
    
        const current = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: server.id } });
        if (current?.status === "RUNNING") {
          try {
            const startUpid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/start`, {});
            await conn.waitForTask(startUpid);
          } catch {  }
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
          const pw = serverRootPassword(current ?? server);
          if (pw && server.vmid) {
            const ready = await waitForGuestAgent(conn, conn.node, server.vmid);
            if (ready) {
              const applied = await applyPasswordToGuest(conn, server.vmid, pw);
              if (!applied) {
                console.error(
                  `[worker] snapshot rollback: could not resync guest password for server ${server.id} (vmid ${server.vmid})`
                );
              }
            } else {
              console.error(
                `[worker] snapshot rollback: guest agent never answered for server ${server.id} (vmid ${server.vmid}) — password may now be stale`
              );
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    
    async function passwordReset(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      if (SANDBOX) {
    
        const password = generatePassword();
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { rootPassword: null, rootPasswordEnc: encryptSecret(password) } });
        await notify(
          server.userId,
          `Root password reset (sandbox) for ${server.hostname}`,
          "Your new root password is on the server's Overview tab (no real VM was touched).",
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
        );
        return;
      }
      if (!server.vmid) throw new Error("password reset: server has no VM yet");
      const { conn } = await nodeFor(server);
      const password = generatePassword();
    
    
    
    
      const applied = await applyPasswordToGuest(conn, server.vmid, password);
      if (!applied) console.error("[worker] agent set-user-password failed, falling back to cloud-init");
    
      if (!applied) {
        await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/config`, { cipassword: password });
        if (server.status === "RUNNING") {
          const upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/qemu/${server.vmid}/status/reboot`, {});
          await conn.waitForTask(upid);
        }
      }
    
      await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { rootPassword: null, rootPasswordEnc: encryptSecret(password) } });
      await notify(
        server.userId,
        `Root password reset for ${server.hostname}`,
        "Your new root password is available on the server's Overview tab.",
        `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
      );
    }
    
    
    export async function reportJobProgress(jobId: string, progress: number, step: string) {
      try {
        await db.job.update({
          where: { id: jobId },
          data: { progress: Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(progress))), step },
        });
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[worker] job progress update failed", e);
      }
    }
    
    
    async function sandboxAnimateJob(
      jobId: string,
      steps: { pct: number; step: string }[],
      delayMs = 500
    ) {
      for (const s of steps) {
        await reportJobProgress(jobId, s.pct, s.step);
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
      }
    }
    
    
    async function backupCreate(job: Job) {
      const server = await loadServer(job);
      if (SANDBOX) {
        await sandboxAnimateJob(job.id, [
          { pct: 5, step: "Starting" },
          { pct: 25, step: "Creating archive… 25%" },
          { pct: 55, step: "Creating archive… 55%" },
          { pct: 80, step: "Creating archive… 80%" },
          { pct: 95, step: "Finalizing" },
          { pct: 100, step: "Backup complete" },
        ]);
    
    
        await notify(
          server.userId,
          `Backup simulated (sandbox) for ${server.hostname}`,
          "Sandbox only — no VM was contacted and no archive was written. This is not a restore point.",
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
        );
        return;
      }
      if (server.vmid == null) {
        throw new Error("backup: this server has no VMID yet, so there is nothing to back up.");
      }
      const vmid = server.vmid;
    
      try {
        await reportJobProgress(job.id, 2, "Starting");
        const { conn, row: nodeRow } = await nodeFor(server);
    
    
    
    
    
        const backupStorage = nodeRow?.isPrimary ? "backup" : (nodeRow?.storageHdd ?? "backup");
    
    
    
        const before = await listBackupsOn(conn, vmid, backupStorage);
    
        const upid = await conn.post(`/nodes/${conn.node}/vzdump`, {
          vmid,
          storage: backupStorage,
          mode: "snapshot",
          compress: "zstd",
        });
        await waitForTaskProgress(
          conn,
          upid,
          (progress, step) => reportJobProgress(job.id, progress, step),
          { timeoutMs: 3_600_000, phase: "backup" }
        );
    
        await reportJobProgress(job.id, 96, "Verifying archive");
        const after = await listBackupsOn(conn, vmid, backupStorage);
        const proof = freshBackupProof({ vmid, before, after });
        if (!proof.proven) throw new Error(`backup not verified — ${proof.reason}`);
    
        await reportJobProgress(job.id, 100, "Backup complete");
    
    
        const keep = resolveBackupKeep(server.backupTier, server.backupKeep);
        try {
          const pruned = await pruneBackups(conn, vmid, keep, backupStorage);
          if (pruned > 0) console.log(`[backup] pruned ${pruned} old backup(s) for vmid ${vmid} (keep ${keep})`);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error(`[backup] prune failed for vmid ${vmid}`, e);
        }
    
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: server.id }, data: { lastAutoBackup: new Date() } });
    
        await notify(
          server.userId,
          `Backup completed for ${server.hostname}`,
          `Verified present in backup storage: ${proof.volid} (${humanBytes(proof.sizeBytes)}).`,
          `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
        );
      } catch (e: any) {
    
    
        if (job.attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
          await notify(
            server.userId,
            `Backup FAILED for ${server.hostname}`,
            `${String(e?.message ?? e).slice(0, 400)} — this server has no new restore point. ` +
              `Your last verified backup (if any) is shown under Backups.`,
            `/dashboard/servers/${server.id}`
          );
        }
        throw e;
      }
    }
    
    const handlers: Record<string, (job: Job) => Promise<void>> = {
      PROVISION: provision,
      START: (j) => powerAction(j, "start"),
      STOP: (j) => powerAction(j, "stop"),

      SNAPSHOT_DELETE: (j) => snapshotOp(j, "delete"),
      SNAPSHOT_ROLLBACK: (j) => snapshotOp(j, "rollback"),
      BACKUP_CREATE: backupCreate,
      BACKUP_RESTORE: backupRestore,
      PASSWORD_RESET: passwordReset,
      SYNC_FORWARDS: async () => { if (!SANDBOX) await syncPortForwards(); },
      SYNC_FIREWALL: syncFirewall,

      GAME_UPDATE: gameUpdate,
      CLAUDE_BUILD: claudeBuild,
      VOLUME_CREATE: volumeCreate,
      VOLUME_ATTACH: volumeAttach,
      VOLUME_DETACH: volumeDetach,

      GAME_CONFIG: gameConfigApply,

      GAME_BACKUP: gameBackup,
      GAME_REINSTALL: gameReinstall,
      GAME_MOD_SYNC: gameModSync,
      GAME_MOD_AUTOUPDATE: gameModAutoUpdate,
      SSL_ISSUE: sslIssue,
      MIGRATE: runMigration,
      DISK_EXPAND: diskExpand,
      ASSIGN_DEDICATED_IP: assignDedicatedIp,
    
      WEBSITE_CREATE: websiteCreate,
      WEBSITE_DELETE: websiteDelete,
      WEBSITE_TOGGLE: websiteToggle,
      WEBSITE_SSL: websiteSsl,

      SITE_UNPUBLISH: siteUnpublish,
      DB_CREATE: dbCreate,
