      CRON_SYNC: cronSync,
      SERVICE_ACTION: serviceAction,
      STAGING_CONTROL: stagingControl,
      ADMIN_OPS: adminOps,
    
    async function reverseCommissionForFailedJob(job: Job, invoiceId: unknown, cents: number, what: string) {
      if (typeof invoiceId !== "string" || !invoiceId || cents <= 0) return;
      await reverseCommissionSafely(`failed ${job.type} job=${job.id}`, () =>
        reversePartnerCommissionForRefund({ invoiceId, refundCents: cents, key: `job:${job.id}`, reason: `refund: failed ${what}` }),
      );
    }
    
    
    async function reconcileFailedDiskExpand(job: Job) {
      const p = (job.payload ?? {}) as { prorateCents?: number; addGB?: number; targetExtraDiskGB?: number; invoiceId?: string };
      const cents = p.prorateCents ?? 0;
      if (cents <= 0 || !job.serverId) return;
    
      const already = await db.auditLog.findFirst({
        where: { action: "server.disk_expand.reconcile", detail: { contains: `job=${job.id}` } },
      });
      if (already) return;
    
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({
        where: { id: job.serverId },
        select: { userId: true, hostname: true, extraDiskGB: true },
      });
      if (!server) return;
    
    
      const target = Number(p.targetExtraDiskGB ?? 0);
      if (target > 0 && server.extraDiskGB >= target) return;
    
      try {
        await db.$transaction([
          db.user.update({ where: { id: server.userId }, data: { balanceCents: { increment: cents } } }),
          db.walletTransaction.create({
            data: {
              userId: server.userId,
              amountCents: cents,
              description: `Refund: failed storage expansion (+${p.addGB ?? "?"} GB) — ${server.hostname}`,
            },
          }),
          db.notification.create({
            data: {
              userId: server.userId,
              title: `Storage expansion failed on ${server.hostname} — refunded`,
              body: `We couldn't add the storage, so $${(cents / 100).toFixed(2)} has been credited back to your wallet. Your server and its data are untouched.`,
              href: `/dashboard/servers/${job.serverId}`,
            },
          }),
        ]);
        await db.auditLog.create({
          data: {
            userId: server.userId,
            action: "server.disk_expand.reconcile",
            detail: `job=${job.id} addGB=${p.addGB} cents=${cents}`,
          },
        });
        await reverseCommissionForFailedJob(job, p.invoiceId, cents, "storage expansion");
      } catch (e: any) {
        await db.auditLog
          .create({
            data: {
              userId: server.userId,
              action: "server.disk_expand.reconcile.uncharged",
              detail: `job=${job.id} cents=${cents} :: ${e?.message ?? e}`,
            },
          })
          .catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    
    
    async function reconcileFailedDedicatedIp(job: Job) {
      const p = (job.payload ?? {}) as { prorateCents?: number; invoiceId?: string };
      const cents = p.prorateCents ?? 0;
      if (!job.serverId) return;
    
      const already = await db.auditLog.findFirst({
        where: { action: "server.dedicated_ip_purchase.reconcile", detail: { contains: `job=${job.id}` } },
      });
      if (already) return;
    
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({
        where: { id: job.serverId },
        select: { userId: true, hostname: true, dedicatedIp: { select: { id: true } } },
      });
      if (!server) return;
      if (server.dedicatedIp) return; // delivered after all — charge stands, nothing to release
    
    
    
      await releaseDedicatedIp(job.serverId).catch((e) =>
        console.error(`[worker] release dedicated IP on reconcile failed for job ${job.id}`, e)
      );
    
      if (cents > 0) {
        try {
          await db.$transaction([
            db.user.update({ where: { id: server.userId }, data: { balanceCents: { increment: cents } } }),
            db.walletTransaction.create({
              data: { userId: server.userId, amountCents: cents, description: `Refund: failed dedicated IP purchase — ${server.hostname}` },
            }),
            db.notification.create({
              data: {
                userId: server.userId,
                title: `Dedicated IP purchase failed on ${server.hostname} — refunded`,
                body: `We couldn't deliver the dedicated IP, so $${(cents / 100).toFixed(2)} has been credited back to your wallet.`,
                href: `/dashboard/servers/${job.serverId}`,
              },
            }),
          ]);
        } catch (e: any) {
          await db.auditLog
            .create({
              data: {
                userId: server.userId,
                action: "server.dedicated_ip_purchase.reconcile.uncharged",
                detail: `job=${job.id} cents=${cents} :: ${e?.message ?? e}`,
              },
            })
            .catch(() => {});
          return;
        }
      }
      await db.auditLog.create({
        data: { userId: server.userId, action: "server.dedicated_ip_purchase.reconcile", detail: `job=${job.id} cents=${cents}` },
      });
      await reverseCommissionForFailedJob(job, p.invoiceId, cents, "dedicated IP purchase");
    }
    
    
    async function reconcileFailedJob(job: Job) {
      if (job.type === "DISK_EXPAND") return reconcileFailedDiskExpand(job);
      if (job.type === "ASSIGN_DEDICATED_IP") return reconcileFailedDedicatedIp(job);
      if (job.type !== "RESIZE") return;
      const p = (job.payload ?? {}) as { prorateCents?: number; direction?: "up" | "down"; invoiceId?: string };
      const cents = p.prorateCents ?? 0;
      if (cents <= 0 || !job.serverId) return;
    
      const already = await db.auditLog.findFirst({
        where: { action: "server.resize.reconcile", detail: { contains: `job=${job.id}` } },
      });
      if (already) return;
    
      const server = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: job.serverId }, select: { userId: true, hostname: true } });
      if (!server) return;
    
      try {
        if (p.direction === "up") {
    
          await db.$transaction([
            db.user.update({ where: { id: server.userId }, data: { balanceCents: { increment: cents } } }),
            db.walletTransaction.create({
              data: { userId: server.userId, amountCents: cents, description: `Refund: failed upgrade — ${server.hostname}` },
            }),
          ]);
        } else if (p.direction === "down") {
    
    
    
          await db.$transaction(async (tx) => {
            await debitWallet(tx, server.userId, cents);
            await tx.walletTransaction.create({
              data: { userId: server.userId, amountCents: -cents, description: `Reversed downgrade credit (resize failed) — ${server.hostname}` },
            });
          });
        }
        await db.auditLog.create({
          data: { userId: server.userId, action: "server.resize.reconcile", detail: `job=${job.id} dir=${p.direction} cents=${cents}` },
        });
    
    
        if (p.direction === "up") await reverseCommissionForFailedJob(job, p.invoiceId, cents, "upgrade");
      } catch (e: any) {
    
        await db.auditLog.create({
          data: { userId: server.userId, action: "server.resize.reconcile.uncharged", detail: `job=${job.id} dir=${p.direction} cents=${cents} :: ${e?.message ?? e}` },
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    
    export async function runJob(job: Job, table: Record<string, (job: Job) => Promise<void>> = handlers) {
    
    
      console.log(`[worker] job ${job.id} ${job.type} server=${job.serverId}`);
      try {
        const handler = table[job.type];
        if (!handler) throw new Error(`No handler for job type ${job.type}`);
        await handler(job);
    
    
    
    
    
        await db.job.update({ where: { id: job.id }, data: { status: "DONE", error: null, finishedAt: new Date() } });
        console.log(`[worker] job ${job.id} DONE`);
      } catch (e: any) {
        let error = `${e?.message ?? e}${e?.body ? ` :: ${e.body}` : ""}`;
    
    
    
    
        if (job.serverId) {
          try {
            const s = await db.server.findUnique({
              where: { id: job.serverId },
              select: { rootPassword: true, rootPasswordEnc: true },
            });
            const pw = s ? serverRootPassword(s) : null;
            if (pw) error = error.split(pw).join("***");
          } catch {
            
          }
        }
        error = error.slice(0, 2000);
        console.error(`[worker] job ${job.id} FAILED: ${error}`);
        const current = await db.job.findUnique({ where: { id: job.id } });
    
    
    
        const retriable = (current?.attempts ?? MAX_ATTEMPTS) < MAX_ATTEMPTS && !isNoRetryError(e);
        await db.job.update({
          where: { id: job.id },
          data: { status: retriable ? "QUEUED" : "FAILED", error, ...(retriable ? {} : { finishedAt: new Date() }) },
        });
        if (!retriable) {
          await reconcileFailedJob(job).catch((err) => console.error(`[worker] reconcile ${job.id} failed`, err));
    
    
    
          await logError(`job.${String(job.type).toLowerCase()}`, new Error(error), {
            source: "job",
            jobId: job.id,
            serverId: job.serverId ?? undefined,
          }).catch(() => {});
          if (job.serverId) {
            const stillExists = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: job.serverId } });
            if (stillExists) {
    
    
    
    
    
    
              if (VM_STATE_FAILURE_JOBS.has(job.type)) {
                await db.server.update({ where: { id: job.serverId }, data: { status: "ERROR" } });
              }
    
    
              if (job.type === "PROVISION") {
                await cleanupFailedProvision(job.serverId);
    
                await notifyProvisionFailed(stillExists, error).catch((err) =>
                  console.error(`[worker] notify provision failure ${job.id} failed`, err)
                );
    
    
    
                await recordDeployFunnelError({
                  product: stillExists.gamePlanId ? "game" : "vps",
                  step: "Provision",
                  stepIndex: 9,
                  errorCode: "PROVISION_FAILED",
                  errorMessage: (error.split("\n")[0] || error).slice(0, 300),
                  userId: stillExists.userId,
                });
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    
    
    async function cleanupFailedProvision(serverId: string): Promise<void> {
      if (SANDBOX) return;
    
      const dedicated = await db.dedicatedIp.findUnique({ where: { serverId }, select: { id: true } });
      await releaseDedicatedIp(serverId).catch((err) =>
        console.error(`[worker] release dedicated IP on failed provision for ${serverId}`, err)
      );
      if (dedicated) {
        await db.server.update({ where: { id: serverId }, data: { ipAddress: null } }).catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    
    
    const MAX_RESTART_RESUMES = Math.max(1, Number(process.env.WORKER_MAX_RESTART_RESUMES ?? 3));
    
    
    function restartCount(job: Pick<Job, "payload">): number {
      const p = job.payload;
      if (p && typeof p === "object" && !Array.isArray(p)) {
        const n = Number((p as Record<string, unknown>)._restarts);
        return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
      }
      return 0;
    }
    function withRestartCount(job: Pick<Job, "payload">, n: number): Prisma.InputJsonValue | undefined {
      const p = job.payload;
      if (p == null) return { _restarts: n };
      if (typeof p === "object" && !Array.isArray(p)) return { ...(p as Record<string, unknown>), _restarts: n } as Prisma.InputJsonValue;
      return undefined; // non-object payload: leave it alone, counter stays 0
    }
    
    
    export async function requeueInterruptedJob(job: Job, why: string): Promise<"QUEUED" | "FAILED"> {
      const restarts = restartCount(job) + 1;
      if (restarts <= MAX_RESTART_RESUMES) {
        const payload = withRestartCount(job, restarts);
    
        await db.$executeRaw`
          UPDATE "Job"
          SET status = 'QUEUED'::"JobStatus",
              attempts = GREATEST(attempts - 1, 0),
              error = NULL,
              step = ${`Resuming after ${why}…`},
              ${payload !== undefined ? Prisma.sql`payload = ${JSON.stringify(payload)}::jsonb,` : Prisma.empty}
              "updatedAt" = now()
          WHERE id = ${job.id} AND status = 'RUNNING'::"JobStatus"`;
        return "QUEUED";
      }
      await db.job.update({
        where: { id: job.id },
        data: { status: "FAILED", error: `Worker restarted mid-job ${restarts} times (${why})`, finishedAt: new Date() },
      });
      await reconcileFailedJob(job).catch((err) => console.error(`[worker] reconcile ${job.id} failed`, err));
      if (job.serverId) {
        const s = await db.server.findUnique({ where: { id: job.serverId } });
        if (s && ["PROVISIONING", "REINSTALLING", "DELETING"].includes(s.status)) {
          await db.server.update({ where: { id: job.serverId }, data: { status: "ERROR" } });
        }
    
    
    
        if (job.type === "PROVISION") {
          await cleanupFailedProvision(job.serverId).catch((err) =>
            console.error(`[worker] cleanup failed provision (reap) ${job.id}`, err)
          );
          if (s) {
            await notifyProvisionFailed(s, "Worker restarted mid-job").catch((err) =>
              console.error(`[worker] notify provision failure (reap) ${job.id} failed`, err)
            );
          }
        }
      }
      return "FAILED";
    }
    
    
    export async function reapStuckJobs() {
      const stuck = await db.job.findMany({ where: { status: "RUNNING" } });
      for (const job of stuck) {
        const outcome = await requeueInterruptedJob(job, "worker restart");
        console.log(`[worker] reaped stuck job ${job.id} (${job.type}) -> ${outcome}`);
      }
    }
    
    
    
    async function dedicatedIpPoolSync(): Promise<void> {
      const nodes = await db.node.findMany({ where: { enabled: true }, select: { id: true } });
      let totalImported = 0;
      let totalCount = 0;
      for (const n of nodes) {
        if (!(await dedicatedIpProviderConfigured(n.id))) continue;
        const r = await syncDedicatedIpPool(n.id);
        totalImported += r.imported;
        totalCount += r.total;
      }
      if (totalImported > 0) console.log(`[worker] dedicated IP pool: imported ${totalImported}, total ${totalCount}`);
    
    
      const { getSettingBool, getSettingInt } = await import("../lib/settings");
      if (await getSettingBool("dedicated_ip_autoreplenish", false)) {
    
    
    
        const free = await freeDedicatedIpCount();
        const threshold = await getSettingInt("dedicated_ip_replenish_threshold", 2);
        if (free < threshold) {
          const recent = await db.notification.findFirst({
            where: { title: { contains: "Dedicated IP pool low" }, createdAt: { gt: new Date(Date.now() - 6 * 3600_000) } },
          });
          if (!recent) {
            const admins = await db.user.findMany({ where: { role: "ADMIN" }, select: { id: true } });
            await db.notification.createMany({
              data: admins.map((a) => ({
                userId: a.id,
                title: `Dedicated IP pool low (${free} free)`,
                body: `Free dedicated IPs (${free}) fell below the replenish threshold (${threshold}). Order more failover IPs from OVH, then the pool auto-imports on the next sync.`,
                href: "/admin/ips",
              })),
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    export type WorkerLoopOptions = {
      limits: SchedulerLimits;
      pollMs: number;
      
      handlers?: Record<string, (job: Job) => Promise<void>>;
      
      select?: typeof selectClaims;
      log?: (msg: string) => void;
    };
    
    export class WorkerLoop {
      readonly active = new Map<string, { promise: Promise<void>; entry: ActiveEntry; job: Job }>();
      private draining = false;
      private stopped = false;
      private loopDone: Promise<void> | null = null;
      private readonly runOne: (job: Job) => Promise<void>;
    
      constructor(private readonly opts: WorkerLoopOptions) {
        const h = opts.handlers;
        this.runOne = h ? (job) => runJob(job, h) : (job) => runJob(job);
      }
    
      get activeCount(): number {
        return this.active.size;
      }
    
      
      private launch(job: Job, candidate: Candidate) {
        const p = this.runOne(job)
          .catch((e) => {
    
    
    
    
    
            console.error(`[worker] job ${job.id} threw outside its own error handling`, e);
          })
          .finally(() => {
            this.active.delete(job.id);
          });
    
    
    
    
        this.active.set(job.id, { promise: p, entry: activeEntryFor(candidate), job });
      }
    
      
      async tick(): Promise<number> {
        const { limits } = this.opts;
        if (this.draining) return 0;
        const freeSlots = limits.concurrency - this.active.size;
        if (freeSlots <= 0) return 0;
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
    
        const take = claimBatchSize(limits);
        const include = { server: { select: { nodeId: true, userId: true } } } as const;
        const interactive = [...INTERACTIVE_TYPES] as JobType[];
        const bulk = [...BULK_TYPES] as JobType[];
        const [ci, cn, cb] = await Promise.all([
          db.job.findMany({ where: { status: "QUEUED", type: { in: interactive } }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" }, take, include }),
          db.job.findMany({ where: { status: "QUEUED", type: { notIn: [...interactive, ...bulk] } }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" }, take, include }),
          db.job.findMany({ where: { status: "QUEUED", type: { in: bulk } }, orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" }, take, include }),
        ]);
        const candidates = [...ci, ...cn, ...cb];
        if (candidates.length === 0) return 0;
    
        const decision = (this.opts.select ?? selectClaims)(
          candidates,
          [...this.active.values()].map((a) => a.entry),
          limits
        );
    
        let claimed = 0;
        for (const candidate of decision.claim) {
          if (this.draining) break;
    
    
    
          const won = await db.$executeRaw`
            UPDATE "Job"
            SET status = 'RUNNING'::"JobStatus",
                attempts = attempts + 1,
                "startedAt" = COALESCE("startedAt", now()),
                "updatedAt" = now()
            WHERE id = ${candidate.id} AND status = 'QUEUED'::"JobStatus"`;
          if (won !== 1) continue; // lost the race (retry path / another process) — move on
          const claimedJob = await db.job.findUnique({ where: { id: candidate.id } });
          if (!claimedJob) continue;
          claimed++;
          const waitMs = Date.now() - claimedJob.createdAt.getTime();
          (this.opts.log ?? console.log)(
            `[worker] job ${claimedJob.id} ${claimedJob.type} server=${claimedJob.serverId} claimed ` +
              `(${this.active.size + 1}/${limits.concurrency} slots busy, ${PRIORITY_LABEL[priorityOf(claimedJob.type)]}, ` +
              `waited ${Math.round(waitMs / 1000)}s)`
          );
          this.launch(claimedJob, candidate);
        }
        return claimed;
      }
    
      
      start(): Promise<void> {
        if (this.loopDone) return this.loopDone;
        this.loopDone = (async () => {
          while (!this.stopped) {
            let claimed = 0;
            try {
              claimed = await this.tick();
            } catch (e) {
              console.error("[worker] loop error", e);
            }
            if (this.stopped) break;
            if (claimed === 0) {
    
    
    
              await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, this.opts.pollMs));
            }
          }
        })();
        return this.loopDone;
      }
    
      
      async stop(drainMs: number): Promise<string[]> {
        this.draining = true;
        const deadline = Date.now() + drainMs;
        while (this.active.size > 0 && Date.now() < deadline) {
          const remaining = Math.max(0, deadline - Date.now());
          await Promise.race([
            Promise.allSettled([...this.active.values()].map((a) => a.promise)),
            new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, Math.min(1000, remaining))),
          ]);
        }
        const requeued: string[] = [];
        for (const [id, a] of this.active) {
          const outcome = await requeueInterruptedJob(a.job, "worker restart").catch((e) => {
            console.error(`[worker] drain: could not requeue ${id}`, e);
            return "FAILED" as const;
          });
          if (outcome === "QUEUED") requeued.push(id);
          console.log(`[worker] drain: ${id} (${a.job.type}) still running -> ${outcome}`);
        }
        this.stopped = true;
        return requeued;
      }
    }
    
    
    const DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS = Math.max(1000, Number(process.env.WORKER_DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 60_000));
    
    const SWEEP_CONCURRENCY = Math.max(1, Number(process.env.WORKER_SWEEP_CONCURRENCY ?? 3));
    
    async function main() {
      console.log("[worker] Nxeon provisioning worker starting");
      await reapStuckJobs();
    
    
    
      if (!SANDBOX) {
        const natErr = await syncPortForwardsBestEffort("worker startup");
        console.log(natErr ? `[worker] port forwards NOT verified: ${natErr.message}` : "[worker] port forwards synced");
      }
    
      const limits = limitsFromEnv();
      console.log(
        `[worker] limits: concurrency=${limits.concurrency} bulkCap=${limits.bulkCap} perNode=${limits.perNodeCap} ` +
          `perNodeHeavy=${limits.perNodeHeavyCap} perUserSoft=${limits.perUserSoft} sweeps=${SWEEP_CONCURRENCY} drain=${DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS}ms`
      );
    
      const sweeps = new SweepRunner({ maxConcurrent: SWEEP_CONCURRENCY });
      registerSweeps(sweeps);
      sweeps.start();
    
      const loop = new WorkerLoop({ limits, pollMs: POLL_MS });
    
    
      const status = setInterval(() => {
        const over = sweeps.stats().filter((s) => s.overruns > 0).map((s) => `${s.name}×${s.overruns}`);
        console.log(
          `[worker] status: ${loop.activeCount}/${limits.concurrency} job slots, ${sweeps.activeCount} sweeps running` +
            (over.length ? `, overruns: ${over.join(" ")}` : "")
        );
      }, 5 * 60_000);
      status.unref();
    
    
    
      let shuttingDown = false;
      const shutdown = async (signal: string) => {
        if (shuttingDown) return;
        shuttingDown = true;
        console.log(`[worker] ${signal} received — draining (${loop.activeCount} in flight, up to ${Math.round(DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000)}s)`);
        clearInterval(status);
        const [requeued, stillSweeping] = await Promise.all([
          loop.stop(DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS),
          sweeps.stop(Math.min(DRAIN_TIMEOUT_MS, 30_000)),
        ]);
        console.log(
          `[worker] drained: ${requeued.length} job(s) requeued for the next process` +
            (stillSweeping.length ? `, sweeps still running at exit: ${stillSweeping.join(", ")}` : "")
        );
        await db.$disconnect().catch(() => {});
        process.exit(0);
      };
      process.on("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown("SIGTERM"));
      process.on("SIGINT", () => void shutdown("SIGINT"));
    
      await loop.start();
    }
    
    
    if (!process.env.NXEON_WORKER_NO_MAIN) main();
